- dataviz: Use this skill whenever you are about to create ANY chart, graph, plot, dashboard, or data visualization, in ANY output medium — an HTML or React artifact, inline SVG, plotting code in any library (matplotlib, plotly, d3, Recharts, …), an image/PNG you will render and upload, or a chart shared into Slack. Read it BEFORE writing the first line of chart code, choosing chart colors, building a stat tile / meter / KPI row, or laying out a dashboard. Produces visualizations that read as one system — elegant, accessible, consistent in light and dark — using a brand-neutral placeholder palette you swap for your own. Teaches a design-system-agnostic method: a form heuristic, a color formula with a runnable validator, mark specs, and interaction rules. A validated default palette is documented in `references/palette.md` — swap that file's values for your brand's. Triggers on: "chart", "graph", "plot", "data viz", "visualization", "dashboard", "analytics", "visualize data", "categorical colors", "sequential / diverging palette", "stat tile", "sparkline", "heatmap", "legend", "axis", "tooltip", "chart colors", "color by series".
- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors ("from now on when X", "each time X", "whenever X", "before/after X") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions ("allow X", "add permission", "move permission to"), env vars ("set X=Y"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: "allow npm commands", "add bq permission to global settings", "move permission to user settings", "set DEBUG=true", "when claude stops show X". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.
- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: "rebind ctrl+s", "add a chord shortcut", "change the submit key", "customize keybindings".
- simplify: Review the changed code for reuse, simplification, efficiency, and altitude cleanups, then apply the fixes. Quality only — it does not hunt for bugs; use /code-review for that.
- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.
- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo, defaults to 10m) - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. "check the deploy every 5 minutes", "keep running /babysit-prs"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.
- claude-api: Reference for the Claude API / Anthropic SDK — model ids, pricing, params, streaming, tool use, MCP, agents, caching, token counting, model migration.
TRIGGER — read BEFORE opening the target file; don't skip because it "looks like a one-liner" — whenever: the prompt names Claude/Anthropic in any form (Claude, Anthropic, Fable, Opus, Sonnet, Haiku, `anthropic`, `@anthropic-ai`, `claude-*`, `us.anthropic.*`, `[1m]`); the user asks about an LLM (pricing/model choice/limits/caching) — never answer from memory; OR the task is LLM-shaped with provider unstated (agent/MCP/tool-definition/multi-agent/RAG/LLM-judge/computer-use; generate/summarize/extract/classify/rewrite/converse over NL; debugging refusals/cutoffs/streaming/tool-calls/tokens).
SKIP only when another provider is being worked on (overrides all triggers): OpenAI/GPT/Gemini/Llama/Mistral/Cohere/Ollama named in the query; OR `grep -rE 'openai|langchain_openai|google.generativeai|genai|mistralai|cohere|ollama'` over the project hits (run this grep FIRST if no provider named — don't Read the file).
- run: Launch and drive this project's app to see a change working. Use when asked to run, start, or screenshot the app, or to confirm a change works in the real app (not just tests). First looks for a project skill that already covers launching the app; otherwise falls back to built-in patterns per project type (CLI, server, TUI, Electron, browser-driven, library).
- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation
- review: Review a GitHub pull request; for your working diff use /code-review
- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch

103	/** 整图硬 alpha：alpha ≥128 → 255，<128 → 0（并清零 RGB），消除提取 PNG 的半透明镶边 */
104	function hardAlpha(img: HTMLImageElement): HTMLCanvasElement {
105	  const c = document.createElement('canvas');
106	  c.width = img.width; c.height = img.height;
107	  const ctx = c.getContext('2d')!;
108	  ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0);
109	  const d = ctx.getImageData(0, 0, c.width, c.height);
110	  const px = d.data;
111	  for (let i = 0; i < px.length; i += 4) {
112	    if (px[i + 3] >= 128) px[i + 3] = 255;
113	    else {
114	      px[i] = 0; px[i + 1] = 0; px[i + 2] = 0; px[i + 3] = 0;
115	    }
116	  }
117	  ctx.putImageData(d, 0, 0);
118	  return c;
119	}
120	
121	export class SpriteAtlas {
122	  data = atlasJson as unknown as AtlasData;
123	  resources = resourcesJson as unknown as ResourcesData;
124	  vanilla = vanillaJson as unknown as VanillaData;
125	  images = new Map<string, HTMLImageElement | HTMLCanvasElement>();
126	  vimages = new Map<string, HTMLImageElement>(); // 原版 PNG（干净像素，不做 hardAlpha）
127	  /** UI 贴图（vanilla-ui/，干净像素不 hardAlpha——UI 有抗锯齿边缘） */
128	  uiimages = new Map<string, HTMLImageElement>();
129	  private uiFiles = (vanillaUiJson as { files: Record<string, string> }).files;
130	  /** 人工标注（annotator.html 导出）：sheet → spriteName → 方位标签 */
131	  annotations: Record<string, Record<string, string>> = {};
132	
133	  async load(onProgress?: (p: number) => void): Promise<void> {
134	    // 封面/Splash_*:Maples 源包的 1920×1080 启动插画(每张 ~17MB 解码 + hardAlpha
135	    // canvas 拷贝),全仓无消费方(菜单用 vanilla-ui/Logo)——启动即死重,跳过
136	    const files = Object.keys(this.data.files).filter((f) => !/封面\/Splash_/.test(f));
137	    // 原版 vanilla 素材与 vanilla-ui 贴图全部不在启动预载(8550 请求/主菜单 2GB 根因):
138	    // 图块/墙/NPC 表 → Game.newWorld/loadWorld 里 preloadVanillaWorld() 预载
139	    // (onWorldReady 之前完成,首帧 chunk 烘焙无回退);物品图标 → vicon 按需
140	    // 懒加载 + 进世界后 prefetchIcons() 后台补齐;UI 贴图 → vui() 按需
141	    // 懒加载(全部 11 处消费方每帧重查,首帧 null 自兜底)
142	    const vfiles: string[] = [];
143	    const uifiles: string[] = [];
144	    let done = 0;
145	    const total = files.length + vfiles.length + uifiles.length;
146	    await Promise.all([
147	      ...files.map((f) => new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
148	        const img = new Image();
149	        img.onload = () => {
150	          // 根源处理：整图硬 alpha —— 抗锯齿半透明像素（提取 PNG 的灰/黑镶边来源）
151	          // 二值化为 0/255，所有消费方（tile/墙/图标/角色）统一获得干净像素
152	          this.images.set(f, hardAlpha(img));
153	          done++;
154	          onProgress?.(done / total);
155	          resolve();
156	        };
157	        img.onerror = () => resolve();
158	        img.src = `sprites/${encodeURI(f)}`;
159	      })),
160	    ]);
161	    // 人工标注（可选，缺失时回退）
162	    try {
163	      const r = await fetch('sprites/annotations.json');
164	      if (r.ok) this.annotations = await r.json();
165	    } catch { /* 无标注 */ }
166	  }
167	
168	  // ---- 原版素材 API（无 Unity y 翻转，按 TEdit 网格寻址） ----
169	
170	  /** 原版图块元数据 */
171	  vmeta(sheetId: number): VanillaTileMeta | null {
172	    return this.vanilla.tiles[String(sheetId)] ?? null;
173	  }
174	
175	  /** 原版图块表取帧（col,row 从 0 起）。越界/缺失返回 null */
176	  vframe(sheetId: number, col: number, row: number): DrawRect | null {
177	    const m = this.vmeta(sheetId);
178	    if (!m) return null;
179	    const img = this.ensureVImage(m.sheet);  // 懒加载兜底(正常路径 preloadVanillaWorld 已就绪)
180	    if (!img) return null;
181	    if (col < 0 || row < 0 || col >= m.cols || row >= m.rows) return null;
182	    return { img, sx: col * m.stride[0], sy: row * m.stride[1], sw: m.grid[0], sh: m.grid[1] };
183	  }
184	
185	  /** 原版表内任意像素偏移取帧（style/显式帧：18px 步长的 frameX/frameY 直用） */
186	  vframeAt(sheetId: number, fx: number, fy: number): DrawRect | null {
187	    const m = this.vmeta(sheetId);
188	    if (!m) return null;
189	    // 与 vframe/vrect 同语义懒加载（ensureVImage）：place_v_* 物品图标走本方法,
190	    // 此前直接 vimages.get——表未载时不发加载请求,宝箱内家具类物品图标永久回退
191	    const img = this.ensureVImage(m.sheet);
192	    if (!img) return null;
193	    if (fx < 0 || fy < 0 || fx + m.grid[0] > img.width || fy + m.grid[1] > img.height) return null;
194	    return { img, sx: fx, sy: fy, sw: m.grid[0], sh: m.grid[1] };
195	  }
196	
197	  /** 原版表内任意矩形（多格物体整体取图，如墓碑 2×2 = 34×34px） */
198	  vrect(sheetId: number, fx: number, fy: number, w: number, h: number): DrawRect | null {
199	    const m = this.vmeta(sheetId);
200	    if (!m) return null;
201	    const img = this.ensureVImage(m.sheet);
202	    if (!img) return null;
203	    if (fx < 0 || fy < 0 || fx + w > img.width || fy + h > img.height) return null;
204	    return { img, sx: fx, sy: fy, sw: w, sh: h };
205	  }
206	
207	  /** 原版 NPC 贴图表取帧（纵向帧条，frameIdx 0-based）。
208	   *  未登记的 id 懒加载 vanilla/NPC_{id}.png（帧数来自 vanilla-npcs.json），首帧返回 null 下一帧生效 */
209	  private lazyNpcMeta = new Map<string, VanillaNpcMeta>();
210	  /** ⚠仅适用【纵向帧条】NPC 表。横向变体横条 NPC（如 594 风气球 = 8 列×32px 变体,
211	   *  Main.cs:23383 Frame(8,1,ai[2])）走此路径会把整条横排画出来——此类 NPC 必须
212	   *  在 Renderer.drawEnemy 加专属分支按列切片（见 drawWindyBalloon）。 */
213	  vnpc(npcId: number, frameIdx: number): DrawRect | null {
214	    let m: VanillaNpcMeta | undefined = this.vanilla.npcs?.[String(npcId)];
215	    if (!m) {
216	      const key = String(npcId);
217	      m = this.lazyNpcMeta.get(key);
218	      if (!m) {
219	        const sheet = `vanilla/NPC_${npcId}.png`;
220	        const img = this.vimages.get(sheet);
221	        if (!img) {
222	          const im = new Image();
223	          im.onload = () => this.vimages.set(sheet, im);
224	          im.src = `sprites/${sheet}`;
225	          return null;
226	        }
227	        const frames = (vanillaNpcFrames as Record<string, number>)[key] ?? 1;
228	        const fh = Math.max(1, Math.floor(img.height / frames));
229	        const meta: VanillaNpcMeta = { sheet, frameW: img.width, frameH: fh, count: frames };
230	        this.lazyNpcMeta.set(key, meta);
231	        m = meta;
232	      }
233	    }
234	    const img = this.vimages.get(m.sheet);
235	    if (!img) return null;
236	    const idx = Math.max(0, Math.min(m.count - 1, frameIdx));
237	    return { img, sx: 0, sy: idx * m.frameH, sw: m.frameW, sh: m.frameH };
238	  }
239	
240	  /** 原版 tile/item 英文名（全量表，未白名单的也有） */
241	  vTileName(id: number): string | null { return this.vanilla.tileNames?.[String(id)] ?? null; }
242	  vItemName(id: number): string | null { return this.vanilla.itemNames?.[String(id)] ?? null; }
243	
244	  vnpcMeta(npcId: number): VanillaNpcMeta | null {
245	    return this.vanilla.npcs?.[String(npcId)] ?? null;
246	  }
247	
248	  /** 原版杂项单图（呼吸气泡等） */
249	  /** 杂项单图(旗帜头像/链条/Boss 部件/Glow 叠画等,全为每帧活画)——
250	   *  miss 走 ensureVImage 触发懒加载:未就绪返回 null,消费方下帧自愈
251	   *  (2026-08-13 前 vimages.get 直取——脱离 VANILLA_MISC 预载即永不出现) */
252	  vmisc(path: string): DrawRect | null {
253	    const hit = this.vimages.get(path); // 命中直接返回(node 测试环境的已注入项同样有效)
254	    if (hit) return { img: hit, sx: 0, sy: 0, sw: hit.width, sh: hit.height };
255	    if (typeof Image === 'undefined') return null; // node 测试环境:无 Image,不触发加载
256	    const img = this.ensureVImage(path);
257	    if (!img) return null;
258	    return { img, sx: 0, sy: 0, sw: img.width, sh: img.height };
259	  }
260	
261	  /** UI 贴图（vanilla-ui/ 命名空间）。name 为白名单键，如 'UI_PanelBackground'。
262	   *  按需懒加载(消费方每帧重查,未就绪返回 null 自兜底) */
263	  vui(name: string): DrawRect | null {
264	    const path = this.uiFiles[name];
265	    if (!path) return null;
266	    const img = this.ensureUiImage(path);
267	    if (!img) return null;
268	    return { img, sx: 0, sy: 0, sw: img.width, sh: img.height };
269	  }
270	
271	  private _uiPending = new Set<string>();
272	  private ensureUiImage(file: string): HTMLImageElement | null {
273	    const hit = this.uiimages.get(file);
274	    if (hit) return hit;
275	    if (this._uiPending.has(file)) return null;
276	    this._uiPending.add(file);
277	    const im = new Image();
278	    im.onload = () => { this.uiimages.set(file, im); this._uiPending.delete(file); };
279	    im.onerror = () => { this._uiPending.delete(file); };
280	    im.src = `sprites/${encodeURI(file)}`;
281	    return null;
282	  }
283	
284	  /** 原版物品图标。构建期已 shelf-pack 进少数 Item_Atlas_k.png 图集(ix/iy/iw/ih
285	   *  子矩形,见 scripts/vanilla-atlas.mjs);旧单体条目(无矩形字段)回退整图语义。
286	   *  未加载时触发后台懒加载并返回 null(下帧生效) */
287	  vicon(itemId: number): DrawRect | null {
288	    const m = this.vanilla.items[String(itemId)];
289	    if (!m) return null;
290	    const img = this.ensureVImage(m.icon);
291	    if (!img) return null;
292	    if (m.ix !== undefined && m.iw !== undefined && m.ih !== undefined) {
293	      return { img, sx: m.ix, sy: m.iy ?? 0, sw: m.iw, sh: m.ih };
294	    }
295	    return { img, sx: 0, sy: 0, sw: img.width, sh: img.height };
296	  }
297	
298	  private _iconPending = new Set<string>();
299	  private _worldPreloaded = false;
300	  /** 懒加载完成回调(Game 注册 → ChunkCache 全量标脏:晚到的表重新烘焙
301	   *  已缓存的 chunk,否则 fallback 会永久烤进 canvas) */
302	  onVImageLoaded: ((file: string) => void) | null = null;
303	
304	  /** 预载文件清单(去重+decode)。tile/wall/NPC/misc 表与图标的统一底层 */
305	  async preloadFiles(files: Iterable<string>, onProgress?: (p: number) => void): Promise<void> {
306	    const list = [...new Set(files)];
307	    let done = 0;
308	    await Promise.all(list.map((f) => new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
309	      if (this.vimages.has(f)) { done++; onProgress?.(done / list.length); return resolve(); }
310	      const img = new Image();
311	      img.onload = () => {
312	        this.vimages.set(f, img);
313	        // decode():预载阶段完成解码,否则 Chrome 延迟到首次 draw 才解码,
314	        // 大表(2000px 级)首帧仍会卡一拍——"预载"语义要求字节+解码双就绪
315	        img.decode().then(() => { done++; onProgress?.(done / list.length); resolve(); }, () => resolve());
316	        this.onVImageLoaded?.(f);
317	      };
318	      img.onerror = () => resolve();
319	      img.src = `sprites/${encodeURI(f)}`;
320	    })));
321	  }
322	
323	  /** 按图块 sheet id + 墙 id 预载对应贴图表。
324	   *  Game 用出生点区域类型扫描调用——只载画面涉及的表(出生点半径内实测仅
325	   *  22/378 张图块表),而不是全量 ~750 张(~250MB 解码) */
326	  preloadTileSheetsFor(tileSheets: Iterable<number>, wallIds: Iterable<number>): Promise<void> {
327	    const files = new Set<string>();
328	    for (const id of tileSheets) {
329	      const m = this.vanilla.tiles[String(id)];
330	      if (m) files.add(m.sheet);
331	    }
332	    for (const id of wallIds) {
333	      const m = this.vanilla.walls[String(id)];
334	      if (m) files.add(m.sheet);
335	    }
336	    return this.preloadFiles(files);
337	  }
338	
339	  /** 预载常驻杂项(树冠/液体/瀑布/电路)+ NPC 表(小动物)——出生点必有,量小全载 */
340	  preloadMiscAndNpcs(): Promise<void> {
341	    return this.preloadFiles([
342	      ...VANILLA_MISC,
343	      ...Object.values(this.vanilla.npcs ?? {}).map((n) => n.sheet),
344	    ]);
345	  }
346	
347	  /** 预载世界渲染所需原版表(全量,~750 张)。仅调试/兜底用;正常路径走
348	   *  preloadTileSheetsFor + preloadMiscAndNpcs 的出生点子集 */
349	  async preloadVanillaWorld(): Promise<void> {
350	    if (this._worldPreloaded) return;
351	    this._worldPreloaded = true;
352	    await Promise.all([
353	      this.preloadTileSheetsFor(
354	        Object.keys(this.vanilla.tiles).map(Number),
355	        Object.keys(this.vanilla.walls).map(Number),
356	      ),
357	      this.preloadMiscAndNpcs(),
358	    ]);
359	  }
360	  /** 按需加载 vanilla 单图(去重;失败静默)。命中返回元素,否则 null。
361	   *  渲染路径一律用此入口(经 vimg 别名)——直接 vimages.get 会绕过懒加载与
362	   *  onVImageLoaded 重烘焙钩子(棕榈树干传送消失的根因) */
363	  ensureVImage(file: string): HTMLImageElement | null {
364	    const hit = this.vimages.get(file);
365	    if (hit) return hit;
366	    if (this._iconPending.has(file) || this._vImageFailed.has(file)) return null;
367	    this._iconPending.add(file);
368	    const im = new Image();
369	    im.onload = () => {
370	      this.vimages.set(file, im);
371	      this._iconPending.delete(file);
372	      this._vImageFailed.delete(file);
373	      this.onVImageLoaded?.(file);  // 晚到的表 → ChunkCache 重烘焙
374	    };
375	    im.onerror = () => {
376	      this._iconPending.delete(file);
377	      this._vImageFailed.add(file);   // 永久失败:不再重复请求,供消费方分级告警
378	    };
379	    im.src = `sprites/${encodeURI(file)}`;
380	    return null;
381	  }
382	  /** 表是否已确认加载失败(404 等)——与"加载中瞬态"区分 */
383	  vImageFailed(file: string): boolean { return this._vImageFailed.has(file); }
384	  private _vImageFailed = new Set<string>();
385	  /** 后台预取全部物品图标(保留兼容入口;预载流程请用 awaited 的 preloadIcons) */
386	  prefetchIcons(): void {
387	    void this.preloadIcons();
388	  }
389	
390	  private _iconsPromise: Promise<void> | null = null;
391	  /** 预载全部物品图标素材。图集化后清单 = 去重后的 ~3 张 Item_Atlas_k.png
392	   *  (此前 6059 张单体逐张请求);缓存 Promise——并发 await 的调用者
393	   *  都会等到同一批加载完成(此前旗标早退会让第二个调用者拿到假完成) */
394	  preloadIcons(onProgress?: (p: number) => void): Promise<void> {
395	    if (this._iconsPromise) return this._iconsPromise;
396	    const icons = [...new Set(Object.values(this.vanilla.items).map((m) => m.icon))];
397	    let done = 0;
398	    this._iconsPromise = Promise.all(icons.map((f) => new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
399	      if (this.vimages.has(f)) { done++; onProgress?.(done / icons.length); return resolve(); }
400	      const im = new Image();
401	      im.onload = () => { this.vimages.set(f, im); done++; onProgress?.(done / icons.length); resolve(); };
402	      im.onerror = () => resolve();
403	      im.src = `sprites/${encodeURI(f)}`;
404	    }))).then(() => undefined);
405	    return this._iconsPromise;
406	  }
407	
408	  /** 预载 UI 贴图按 key 前缀(如 ['Player_'] = 纸娃娃身体/发型,545 张)。
409	   *  exclude:子族前缀排除表(如 'UI_Bestiary')——面板专属子族只在面板打开时
410	   *  由 vui 懒加载自愈,不进启动/进图预载(2026-08-13 UI_ 397 键收窄用)。 */
411	  async preloadUiPrefix(prefixes: string[], onProgress?: (p: number) => void, exclude?: string[]): Promise<void> {
412	    const files = Object.entries(this.uiFiles)
413	      .filter(([k]) => prefixes.some((p) => k.startsWith(p))
414	        && !(exclude ?? []).some((e) => k.startsWith(e)))
415	      .map(([, f]) => f);
416	    return this.preloadUiFiles(files, onProgress);
417	  }
418	
419	  /** 按 UI 文件路径预载(装备中的具体 Armor 表;decode 保证首帧无解码卡顿) */
420	  async preloadUiFiles(files: Iterable<string>, onProgress?: (p: number) => void): Promise<void> {
421	    const list = [...new Set(files)];
422	    let done = 0;
423	    await Promise.all(list.map((f) => new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
424	      if (this.uiimages.has(f)) { done++; onProgress?.(done / list.length); return resolve(); }
425	      const im = new Image();
426	      im.onload = () => {
427	        this.uiimages.set(f, im);
428	        im.decode().then(() => { done++; onProgress?.(done / list.length); resolve(); }, () => resolve());
429	      };
430	      im.onerror = () => resolve();
431	      im.src = `sprites/${encodeURI(f)}`;
432	    })));
433	  }
434	
435	  has(file: string): boolean {
436	    return this.images.has(file);
437	  }
438	
439	  /** 取精灵绘制矩形（Unity y 翻转已处理）。找不到返回 null。 */
440	  rect(file: string, sprite: string): DrawRect | null {
441	    const img = this.images.get(file);
442	    const entry = this.data.files[file];
443	    if (!img || !entry) return null;
444	    const s = entry.sprites.find((x) => x.name === sprite);
445	    if (!s) return null;
446	    return { img, sx: s.x, sy: img.height - s.y - s.h, sw: s.w, sh: s.h };
447	  }
448	
449	  animFrames(anim: string): DrawRect[] {
450	    const frames = this.resources.anims[anim] ?? [];
451	    return frames.map((f) => this.rect(f.file, f.sprite)).filter(Boolean) as DrawRect[];
452	  }
453	}
454	
455	// ---- 物品图标：一律原版 Item_N（旧 Maples UI/ 图标层已整体移除） ----
456	import { TILE_BY_KEY, TILE_DEFS } from '../data/tiles';
457	
458	/** DrawAnimationVertical(int.MaxValue, 3) 的静态等价(IsFood 全表) */
459	const FOOD = { dur: Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER, n: 3, static: true };
460	const FOOD_IDS: number[] = [
461	  353, 357, 967, 969, 1787, 1911, 1912, 1919, 1920, 2266, 2267, 2268, 
462	  2425, 2426, 2427, 3195, 3532, 4009, 4010, 4011, 4012, 4013, 4014, 4015, 
463	  4016, 4017, 4018, 4019, 4020, 4021, 4022, 4023, 4024, 4025, 4026, 4027, 
464	  4028, 4029, 4030, 4031, 4032, 4033, 4034, 4035, 4036, 4037, 4282, 4283, 
465	  4284, 4285, 4286, 4287, 4288, 4289, 4290, 4291, 4292, 4293, 4294, 4295, 
466	  4296, 4297, 4403, 4411, 4614, 4615, 4616, 4617, 4618, 4619, 4620, 4621, 
467	  4622, 4623, 4624, 4625, 5009, 5041, 5042, 5092, 5093, 5275, 5277, 5278, 
468	  5537, 5645, 
469	];
470	
471	/**
472	 * 物品贴图动画注册表(Main.InitializeItemAnimations :3685-3722 1:1)。
473	 * 食物族:ItemID.Sets.IsFood(:258,86 项)全部为竖 3 帧条,原版注册
474	 * DrawAnimationVertical(int.MaxValue,3) = 恒帧 0(顶部一帧);本表以 FOOD
475	 * 静态条目等价表达(切片帧高 = 总高/3)。
476	 * 物品源图是竖排多帧条(如坠星 Item_75=22×208),不切片会整条入画。
477	 * dur=tick/帧;pingPong=往返;static=true=恒帧 0(IsFood 的 int.MaxValue
478	 * 静态三帧与 NotActuallyAnimating 族)。
479	 */
480	export const ITEM_ANIMATION: Record<number, { dur: number; n: number; pingPong?: boolean; static?: boolean }> = {
481	  3581: { dur: 4, n: 4 },
482	  3580: { dur: 6, n: 4 },
483	  75: { dur: 5, n: 8, pingPong: true },    // 坠星 8 帧 PingPong 旋转
484	  575: { dur: 6, n: 4 }, 547: { dur: 6, n: 4 }, 520: { dur: 6, n: 4 },
485	  548: { dur: 6, n: 4 }, 521: { dur: 6, n: 4 }, 549: { dur: 6, n: 4 },
486	  3453: { dur: 6, n: 4 }, 3454: { dur: 6, n: 4 }, 3455: { dur: 6, n: 4 },
487	  4068: { dur: 6, n: 4, static: true },   // NotActuallyAnimating(:3701-3709)
488	  4069: { dur: 6, n: 4, static: true },
489	  4070: { dur: 6, n: 4, static: true },
490	  5644: { dur: 7, n: 9 },                 // ScryingOrb(专属着色器,垂直循环近似)
491	  // ---- 食物族(IsFood :258):竖 3 帧条,原版 int.MaxValue 恒帧 0 ----
492	  ...Object.fromEntries(FOOD_IDS.map((v) => [v, FOOD])),
493	};
494	
495	
496	/** 物品动画当前帧(DrawAnimationVertical.GetFrame 语义;tick=游戏 tick 或等价毫秒换算) */
497	export function itemAnimFrame(vid: number, tick: number): number {
498	  const a = ITEM_ANIMATION[vid];
499	  if (!a || a.static) return 0;
500	  const span = a.pingPong ? a.n * 2 - 2 : a.n;
501	  const idx = Math.floor(tick / a.dur) % span;
502	  return a.pingPong && idx >= a.n ? span - idx : idx;
503	}
504	
505	/** 把整条 DrawRect 按帧切片(vid 无动画原样返回) */
506	export function sliceItemAnimFrame(vid: number, ar: DrawRect, tick: number): DrawRect {
507	  const a = ITEM_ANIMATION[vid];
508	  if (!a || a.n <= 1) return ar;
509	  const fh = ar.sh / a.n;
510	  const f = Math.min(a.n - 1, itemAnimFrame(vid, tick));
511	  return { img: ar.img, sx: ar.sx, sy: ar.sy + Math.round(fh * f), sw: ar.sw, sh: Math.round(fh) };
512	}
513	
514	export function atlasIconForKey(atlas: SpriteAtlas, key: string): DrawRect | null {
515	  const vid = VANILLA_ITEM_ICON_MAP[key];
516	  if (vid !== undefined) return atlas.vicon(vid);
517	  // 阶段 5:vi_<id>_* 全量物品回退原版图标
518	  if (key.startsWith('vi_')) {
519	    const id = parseInt(key.slice(3), 10);
520	    if (Number.isFinite(id)) return atlas.vicon(id);
521	  }
522	  // 阶段 5:place_v_* 放置物品回退用图块贴图首帧作图标
523	  if (key.startsWith('place_v_')) {
524	    const tk = key.slice('place_'.length);
525	    const tid = TILE_BY_KEY[tk];
526	    if (tid !== undefined) {
527	      const td = TILE_DEFS[tid];
528	      // vframeAt 而非 vframe:窄条表(如压板 135 的 16×200 竖条)cols=0 会让 vframe 判越界
529	      if (td?.vanilla) return atlas.vframeAt(td.vanilla.sheet, 0, 0) ?? atlas.vframe(td.vanilla.sheet, 0, 0);
530	    }
531	  }
532	  return null;
533	}
534

16	const spriteCache = new Map<number, HTMLImageElement>();
17	export function projSprite(projId: number): HTMLImageElement | null {
18	  let img = spriteCache.get(projId);
19	  if (img !== undefined) return img ?? null;
20	  if (typeof Image === 'undefined') return null;
21	  img = new Image();
22	  img.src = `sprites/vanilla/Projectile_${projId}.png`;
23	  spriteCache.set(projId, img);
24	  return img;

27	/** Main.projFrames（Main.cs:8392 起全 275 条非 1 帧赋值，tools 内联提取）：
28	 *  未列入的恒 1 帧。投射物贴图是【竖向多帧行】——帧高 = 图高/帧数，
29	 *  整图绘制会把多帧压成胶片条（史莱姆随从 266 曾 12 帧压成一坨） */
30	import projFramesJson from '../data/vanilla-projframes.json';
31	import { projectileData } from '../data/vanillaProjectiles';
32	const PROJ_FRAMES = projFramesJson as Record<string, number>;
33	export function projFrameCount(projId: number): number {
34	  return PROJ_FRAMES[String(projId)] ?? 1;
35	}
36	
37	/** 单帧裁切缓存（id+帧号 → canvas），多帧行按帧高切片 */
38	const frameCache = new Map<string, HTMLCanvasElement>();
39	export function projFrameImg(projId: number, frameIdx: number): HTMLCanvasElement | null {
40	  const img = projSprite(projId);
41	  if (!img || !img.complete || img.naturalWidth === 0) return null;
42	  const frames = projFrameCount(projId);
43	  const idx = Math.max(0, Math.min(frames - 1, frameIdx));
44	  const fh = img.naturalHeight / frames;
45	  if (!Number.isFinite(fh) || fh < 1) return null;
46	  const key = `${projId}|${idx}`;
47	  let c = frameCache.get(key);
48	  if (c) return c;
49	  c = document.createElement('canvas');
50	  c.width = img.naturalWidth;
51	  c.height = Math.round(fh);
52	  const cx = c.getContext('2d')!;
53	  cx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
54	  cx.drawImage(img, 0, Math.round(idx * fh), img.naturalWidth, Math.round(fh), 0, 0, c.width, c.height);
55	  if (frameCache.size > 2048) frameCache.clear();
56	  frameCache.set(key, c);
57	  return c;
58	}

172	  /** alpha（255 起 −15/t；线上钳 150 / 线下钳 0——:22197-22206） */
173	  starAlpha = 255;
174	  /** 周期音（Item9 :22177-22180，20-60t 掷一次） */
175	  private starSndT = 20;
176	  /** tileCollide 已生效（越目标线后） */
177	  private starCollide = false;
178	  /** localAI[0] 计时 */
179	  terraT = 0;
180	  /** ai[0] 朝向（±1）/ ai[1] 生命基准 18 / ai[2] 物品 scale（出生注入） */
181	  terraAi0 = 1;
182	  terraAi1 = 18;
183	  terraAi2 = 1;
184	  /** 尾段清伤（t ≥ Lerp(ai1, ai1+25, 0.65) → damage=0，:39337-39340） */
185	  terraNoHit = false;
186	  /** localAI[1] 近墙减速标记 */
187	  terraWall = false;
188	  /** 已反射（原版 Projectile.reflected：反射源命中后置位，防重复反射；
189	   *  反射后弹体转 hostile——不再入下方敌怪判定，改走 hitPlayer 伤玩家） */
190	  reflected = false;
191	  /** 穿透投射物的同敌免疫表（敌人 id 集合） */
192	  private hitSet = new Set<number>();
193	  // ---- 喵刀弹 502（Meowmere Cat）专属态 ----
194	  /** ai[0]：弹跳+命中共享计数（0..5，≥5 消亡）——弹跳 :18169 递增/命中 :16794
195	   *  递增同槽（先到 5 者杀弹，喵叫音高 style=5+ai0 随之爬升） */
196	  meowCount = 0;
197	  /** localNPCHitCooldown 同敌再命中冷却（SetDefaults :5466-5468 = 10t） */
198	  private meowHitCd = new Map<number, number>();
199	  /** oldPos 拖尾环（彩虹拖尾 Main.cs:32495-32513 沿 oldPos 逐段绘制，N=10） */
200	  private meowTrail: number[] = [];
201	  /** 迪斯科 RGB（DoUpdate_AnimateDiscoRGB Main.cs:19441 六段 ±7/帧；初值 R=255） */
202	  private discoR = 255;
203	  private discoG = 0;
204	  private discoB = 0;
205	  private discoStyle = 0;
206	  /** 弹体点光（Game 实体光扫读 lightRGB——同 Enemy 约定） */
207	  lightRGB: [number, number, number] | null = null;
208	  /** 点光源像素覆盖（缺省=实体中心；985 泰拉刃 :39382 光心=弹心+朝向 85×scale） */
209	  lightRGBAt: { x: number; y: number } | null = null;
210	  dead = false;
211	
212	  constructor(x: number, y: number, vx: number, vy: number, damage: number,
213	    knockback: number, projId = 1, dropKey: string | null = null, opts?: ArrowOpts) {
214	    super();
215	    this.x = x; this.y = y;
216	    this.vx = vx; this.vy = vy;
217	    this.damage = damage;
218	    this.knockback = knockback;
219	    this.projId = projId;
220	    this.dropKey = dropKey;
221	    this.grav = opts?.grav ?? 0.3;
222	    this.life = opts?.life ?? 1200;
223	    this.pierce = opts?.pierce ?? 1;
224	    this.pierceInit = this.pierce;
225	    this.hostile = opts?.hostile ?? false;
226	    this.bounce = opts?.bounce ?? false;
227	    this.thornBounce = opts?.thornBounce ?? false;
228	    this.gravDelay = opts?.gravDelay ?? -1;
229	    this.homing = opts?.homing ?? null;
230	    this.extraUpdates = opts?.extraUpdates ?? PROJ_EXTRA_UPDATES[projId] ?? 0;
231	    this.drag = opts?.drag ?? 1;
232	    this.maxFall = opts?.maxFall ?? 16;
233	    this.tumble = opts?.tumble ?? false;
234	    this.tumblePoseLock = opts?.tumblePoseLock ?? false;
235	    this.terra = !!opts?.terra;
236	    if (opts?.terra) {
237	      this.terraAi0 = opts.terra.ai0;
238	      this.terraAi1 = opts.terra.ai1;
239	      this.terraAi2 = opts.terra.ai2;
240	    }
241	    if (opts?.star) this.starY = opts.star.targetY;
242	  }
243	
244	  draw(r: Renderer, _cam: Camera): void {
245	    // 世界坐标绘制(Renderer 实体循环运行在世界变换内,勿再自算屏幕坐标——
246	    // 曾双变换把箭甩出屏幕,表现为"箭隐形但能命中")
247	    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');
248	    if (!ctx) return;
249	    // 泰拉刃光束 985 独占（DrawProj_TerraBlade2Shot Main.cs:27670-27712）：
250	    // Frame(1,4) 四帧 170×170（帧 0 主体/帧 3 白芯）；多层镜像叠画：
251	    // 蓝(45,124,205)×2 对称 ±π/8 张开 + 绿白内芯 ×2 + 绿(34,177,76) ×2
252	    // ±0.25 收拢 + 黄绿(181,230,29) + 帧 3 白芯三层递缩——ai[0]=0（未传参）
253	    // 时各 ±ai0 项归零对称；旋转角=velocity 角（无 +π/2，贴图正交）。
254	    // 淡出 = Remap(localAI[0],0,25,1,0)
255	    if (this.terra) {
256	      const img = projSprite(985);
257	      if (!img || !img.complete || img.naturalWidth === 0) return;
258	      const fw = img.naturalWidth, fh = img.naturalHeight / 4;
259	      // 淡入淡出（:39344 985 专属）：Remap(t,0,ai1/2,0,1)×Remap(t,ai1+13,ai1+25,1,0)
260	      const aIn = Math.min(1, this.terraT / (this.terraAi1 * 0.5));
261	      const aOut = this.terraT <= this.terraAi1 + 13 ? 1
262	        : Math.max(0, (this.terraAi1 + 25 - this.terraT) / 12);
263	      const fade = aIn * aOut;
264	      // num6 双段（:39273 主段 Remap(t,ai1*0.4,ai1+25,0,1)——绘制张合的推进分母）
265	      const num = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, (this.terraT - this.terraAi1 * 0.4) / (this.terraAi1 + 25 - this.terraAi1 * 0.4)));
266	      const spread = 1 - num;
267	      // 绘制 scale（:39341）：Remap(1−(1−num6)²,0,1,1.5,1)×ai2
268	      const fv = 1 - (1 - num) * (1 - num);
269	      const terraScale = (1.5 - 0.5 * Math.min(1, Math.max(0, fv))) * this.terraAi2;
270	      const rot = Math.atan2(this.vy, this.vx);
271	      const layer = (sy: number, a: number, angOff: number, frame: number, flipV: boolean) => {
272	        ctx.save();
273	        ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, a * fade));
274	        ctx.translate(this.cx, this.cy);
275	        ctx.rotate(rot + angOff * this.terraAi0);   // ±ai[0] 方向项（:27694 ai0=±1）
276	        if (flipV) ctx.scale(1, -1);
277	        const dw = fw * sy * terraScale;
278	        ctx.drawImage(img, 0, frame * fh, fw, fh, -dw / 2, -fh * sy * terraScale / 2, dw, fh * sy * terraScale);
279	        ctx.restore();
280	        ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
281	      };
282	      ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
283	      layer(0.95, 0.5, Math.PI / 8 * spread, 0, false);            // 蓝镜像对（±π/8×spread）
284	      layer(0.95, 0.5, -Math.PI / 8 * spread, 0, true);
285	      layer(1, 0.12, 0, 0, false);                                 // 绿白内芯（color5 近似）
286	      layer(1, 0.3, Math.PI / 4 * 0.25 * spread, 0, false);        // 绿 ±0.25 收拢
287	      layer(1, 0.3, -Math.PI / 4 * 0.25 * spread, 0, true);
288	      layer(0.975, 0.5, Math.PI / 4 * 0.15 * spread, 0, false);    // 黄绿主芯
289	      layer(1, 0.6, Math.PI / 4 * 0.05 * spread, 3, false);        // 帧 3 白芯三层
290	      layer(0.8, 0.5, -0.05, 3, false);
291	      layer(0.6, 0.4, -0.1, 3, false);
292	      return;
293	    }
294	    // 星怒剑 503 独占：rotation=vel−π/2（:22236-22239），origin (w/2, 70)
295	    // （Main.cs:29898-29901——34×90 贴图锚点近底部，剑尖前伸 70px）；
296	    // Opacity = 1−alpha/255 渐显
297	    if (this.starY !== null) {
298	      const img = projSprite(503);
299	      if (!img || !img.complete || img.naturalWidth === 0) return;
300	      ctx.save();
301	      ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, 1 - this.starAlpha / 255));
302	      ctx.translate(this.cx, this.cy);
303	      ctx.rotate(Math.atan2(this.vy, this.vx) - Math.PI / 2);
304	      ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
305	      ctx.drawImage(img, -img.naturalWidth / 2, -70, img.naturalWidth, img.naturalHeight);
306	      ctx.restore();
307	      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
308	      return;
309	    }
310	    // 翻滚态用累积角（aiStyle 2）；48/54/93/520/599 平飞期姿态锁定 atan2
311	    const tumbling = this.tumble && !(this.tumblePoseLock && this.age <= this.gravDelay);
312	    const ang = tumbling ? this.tumbleRot : Math.atan2(this.vy, this.vx);
313	    const img = projSprite(this.projId);
314	    // 喵刀弹 502 彩虹拖尾（Main.cs:32495-32513）：Projectile_250.png（14×32）沿
315	    // oldPos 逐段——旋转=段向-π/2、纵向拉伸=段长/32、alpha=127/255×(1-i/N)，
316	    // 画在【本体之前】（原版在本体绘制后叠画，此处先画拖尾再本体等效）
317	    if (this.projId === 502 && this.meowTrail.length >= 4) {
318	      const streak = projSprite(250);
319	      if (streak && streak.complete && streak.naturalWidth > 0) {
320	        const N = this.meowTrail.length / 2;
321	        for (let i = N - 1; i > 0; i--) {
322	          const x1 = this.meowTrail[i * 2], y1 = this.meowTrail[i * 2 + 1];
323	          const x2 = this.meowTrail[(i - 1) * 2], y2 = this.meowTrail[(i - 1) * 2 + 1];
324	          const segLen = Math.hypot(x2 - x1, y2 - y1);
325	          if (segLen < 0.01) continue;
326	          const segAng = Math.atan2(y2 - y1, x2 - x1) - Math.PI / 2;
327	          ctx.save();
328	          ctx.translate(x1 + this.w / 2, y1 + this.h / 2);
329	          ctx.rotate(segAng);
330	          ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
331	          ctx.globalAlpha = (127 / 255) * (1 - i / N);
332	          // origin (w/2, 0)：段起点为锚、沿段向拉伸到段长（scaleY=段长/贴图高）
333	          ctx.drawImage(streak, -streak.naturalWidth / 2, 0,
334	            streak.naturalWidth, Math.max(1, segLen));
335	          ctx.restore();
336	          ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
337	        }
338	      }
339	    }
340	    ctx.save();
341	    ctx.translate(this.x + this.w / 2, this.y + this.h / 2);
342	    ctx.rotate(ang + Math.PI / 2); // 原版贴图纵向：rotation = atan2+π/2（AI_001 L54877）
343	    // 502 朝向（:22659-22665）：spriteDirection=sign(vx)——贴图在旋转坐标系内水平镜像
344	    if (this.projId === 502 && this.vx < 0) ctx.scale(-1, 1);
345	    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
346	    if (img && img.complete && img.naturalWidth > 0) {
347	      ctx.drawImage(img, -this.w / 2, -this.w / 2, this.w, this.w * (img.naturalHeight / img.naturalWidth));
348	    } else {
349	      // 贴图未就绪：短线兜底
350	      ctx.strokeStyle = this.projId === 2 ? '#FFB060' : '#D8C8A0';
351	      ctx.lineWidth = 2;
352	      ctx.beginPath();
353	      ctx.moveTo(0, 0);
354	      ctx.lineTo(0, -Math.min(14, Math.hypot(this.vx, this.vy) * 1.4));
355	      ctx.stroke();
356	    }
357	    ctx.restore();
358	  }
359	
360	  /** 统一消亡出口（Projectile.Kill 语义钩子）：碎镜 1085 近身 150px 坏运等
361	   *  按弹型分发由 Game 侧 onProjectileDeath 消费 */
362	  private killNow(game: GameHooks): void {
363	    this.dead = true;
364	    game.onProjectileDeath?.(this.projId, this.x, this.y, this.w, this.h);
365	  }
366	
367	  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks) {
368	    // 联机傀儡（远端弹幕快照驱动）：对敌判定归拥有者客户端；对玩家（hostile）由访客本地结算 Damage_EVP（netPuppetProjContact）
369	    if (this.netPuppet) { this.netPuppetStep(); return; }
370	    // extraUpdates 子步循环（Projectile.cs:15331-15336）：numUpdates = extraUpdates，
371	    // while(numUpdates>=0) 跑完整 AI/位移/命中体——timeLeft 也在循环内逐子步消耗
372	    //（:15861），故弹体寿命同步缩短为 1/(N+1)。
373	    for (let u = 0; u <= this.extraUpdates; u++) {
374	      this.subStep(game);
375	      if (this.dead) return;
376	    }
377	  }
378	
379	  /** 单次子步（无 extraUpdates 时即整帧本体） */
380	  private subStep(game: GameHooks): void {
381	    if (--this.life <= 0) { this.killNow(game); return; }
382	    const world = game.world;
383	    this.age++;
384	    // aiStyle 1/2 通用重力（原版箭 0.3/tick 抛物线；直飞弹 grav=0）；
385	    // 275/276 延迟重力（Projectile.cs:54318-54329：35t 后 g=0.025）；
386	    // aiStyle 2 投掷族（Projectile.cs:21955-21977 默认档）：20t 平飞后
387	    // vy+=0.4、vx×=0.97，终端 32
388	    if (this.grav !== 0 && (this.gravDelay < 0 || this.age > this.gravDelay)) {
389	      this.vy = Math.min(this.vy + this.grav, this.maxFall);
390	      if (this.drag !== 1) this.vx *= this.drag;
391	    }
392	    // 翻滚（aiStyle 2 刀族 :21508，自出生每 tick 累加）；48/54/93/520/599 在
393	    // 平飞期由 draw 侧姿态锁定覆盖（:21971-21972）
394	    if (this.tumble) {
395	      this.tumbleRot += (Math.abs(this.vx) + Math.abs(this.vy)) * 0.03 * (this.vx >= 0 ? 1 : -1);
396	    }
397	    // 泰拉刃光束 985（AI_191 :39248-39266 + 985 专属尾段 :39333-39377）：
398	    // 寿命 = ai[1]+25（出生 18 → 43t）；淡入 ai[1]/2=9t、末 12t 淡出（:39344）；
399	    // t≥Lerp(ai1,ai1+25,0.65)≈34 清伤（纯视觉尾段）；>8 速才减速/探墙（正牌
400	    // 出生速=瞄准向×5 恒不触发，973 甩剑共用段）
401	    if (this.terra) {
402	      this.terraT++;
403	      const terraLife = this.terraAi1 + 25;
404	      if (this.terraT >= terraLife) { this.killNow(game); return; }
405	      if (!this.terraNoHit && this.terraT >= Math.round(this.terraAi1 + 25 * 0.65)) {
406	        this.terraNoHit = true;
407	        this.damage = 0;                                  // :39337-39340 damage=0
408	      }
409	      const stT = world.store;
410	      this.lightRGB = [0.25, 0.86, 0.38];    // Color(64,220,96)（:39382）
411	      // 光心 = 弹心 + 朝向 ×85×scale（:39382 Center+rotation.ToRotationVector2()*85*scale）
412	      {
413	        const sp = Math.hypot(this.vx, this.vy) || 1;
414	        this.lightRGBAt = { x: this.cx + (this.vx / sp) * 85, y: this.cy + (this.vy / sp) * 85 };
415	      }
416	      if (Math.hypot(this.vx, this.vy) > 0.5 && Math.random() < 0.6) {
417	        game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.cy, '#7CE81E', 1, 1.2, { life: 18 });
418	      }
419	      if (Math.hypot(this.vx, this.vy) > 8) {
420	        this.vx *= 0.94; this.vy *= 0.94;
421	        if (!this.terraWall) {
422	          const rot = Math.atan2(this.vy, this.vx);
423	          let clear = false;
424	          for (let k = -1; k <= 1 && !clear; k += 0.5) {
425	            const a = rot + k * (Math.PI / 4) * 0.25;
426	            clear = canHit(stT, this.cx, this.cy, 0, 0,
427	              this.cx + Math.cos(a) * 110, this.cy + Math.sin(a) * 110, 0, 0);
428	          }
429	          if (!clear) this.terraWall = true;
430	        }
431	        if (this.terraWall && Math.hypot(this.vx, this.vy) > 8) { this.vx *= 0.8; this.vy *= 0.8; }
432	        if (this.terraWall) { this.vx *= 0.88; this.vy *= 0.88; }
433	      }
434	    }
435	    // 星怒剑 503（aiStyle 5 :22139-22157 + 503 段 :22197-22266）：无重力恒速；
436	    // 线上方穿墙（tileCollide=false）→ 越线后撞块；alpha 255−15/t 渐显（线上
437	    // 钳 150 / 线下钳 0）；周期 Item9 星啸；尘 58 星尘尾；rotation=vel−π/2（draw 侧）
438	    if (this.starY !== null) {
439	      // :22139-22143 tileCollide=true 仅当 Center.Y > ai[1]（严格大于——边界对齐）
440	      this.starCollide = this.cy > this.starY;
441	      const above = this.cy < this.starY;
442	      const floorA = above ? 150 : 0;
443	      this.starAlpha = Math.max(floorA, this.starAlpha - 15);
444	      if (--this.starSndT <= 0) {
445	        this.starSndT = 20 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 40);
446	        game.playSfxFiles(['Item_9'], 0.5, this.cx, this.cy);
447	      }
448	      if (Math.random() < 0.3) {
449	        game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.cy, '#FFF8C8', 1, 0.9, { life: 20 });
450	      }
451	    }
452	    // 专家追踪（Projectile.cs:54330-54345 275/276 / :23307-23316 277：
453	    // v=(v*(weight-1)+dirToPlayer*speed)/weight；<floor 或 >cap 归一）
454	    if (this.homing) {
455	      const p = game.player;
456	      if (p && !p.dead) {
457	        let dx = p.cx - (this.x + this.w / 2), dy = p.cy - (this.y + this.h / 2);
458	        const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
459	        dx = dx / d * this.homing.speed;
460	        dy = dy / d * this.homing.speed;
461	        this.vx = (this.vx * (this.homing.weight - 1) + dx) / this.homing.weight;
462	        this.vy = (this.vy * (this.homing.weight - 1) + dy) / this.homing.weight;
463	        const sp = Math.hypot(this.vx, this.vy);
464	        if (this.homing.floor !== undefined && sp < this.homing.floor && sp > 0) {
465	          this.vx = this.vx / sp * this.homing.floor;
466	          this.vy = this.vy / sp * this.homing.floor;
467	        }
468	        if (this.homing.cap !== undefined && sp > this.homing.cap) {
469	          this.vx = this.vx / sp * this.homing.cap;
470	          this.vy = this.vy / sp * this.homing.cap;
471	        }
472	      }
473	    }
474	    this.x += this.vx;
475	    this.y += this.vy;
476	    // 喵刀弹 502：迪斯科点光（Projectile.cs:22613-22621 (Disco+0.5)/2）+ 拖尾采样
477	    if (this.projId === 502) {
478	      const n = 7;   // DoUpdate_AnimateDiscoRGB（Main.cs:19441-19496 六段 ±7/帧）
479	      switch (this.discoStyle) {
480	        case 0: this.discoG = Math.min(255, this.discoG + n); if (this.discoG >= 255) this.discoStyle++; break;
481	        case 1: this.discoR = Math.max(0, this.discoR - n); if (this.discoR <= 0) this.discoStyle++; break;
482	        case 2: this.discoB = Math.min(255, this.discoB + n); if (this.discoB >= 255) this.discoStyle++; break;
483	        case 3: this.discoG = Math.max(0, this.discoG - n); if (this.discoG <= 0) this.discoStyle++; break;
484	        case 4: this.discoR = Math.min(255, this.discoR + n); if (this.discoR >= 255) this.discoStyle++; break;
485	        default: this.discoB = Math.max(0, this.discoB - n); if (this.discoB <= 0) this.discoStyle = 0; break;
486	      }
487	      this.lightRGB = [(0.5 + this.discoR / 255) / 2, (0.5 + this.discoG / 255) / 2, (0.5 + this.discoB / 255) / 2];
488	      this.meowTrail.push(this.x, this.y);
489	      if (this.meowTrail.length > 20) this.meowTrail.splice(0, this.meowTrail.length - 20);   // oldPos 10 点
490	      for (const [k, v] of this.meowHitCd) {
491	        if (v <= 1) this.meowHitCd.delete(k); else this.meowHitCd.set(k, v - 1);
492	      }
493	    }
494	    const tx = Math.floor((this.x + this.w / 2) / TILE);
495	    const ty = Math.floor((this.y + this.h / 2) / TILE);
496	    if (!world.store.inBounds(tx, ty)) { this.killNow(game); return; }
497	    const tileType = world.store.get(tx, ty);

634	  private bounceOff(game: GameHooks): boolean {
635	    const st = game.world.store;
636	    const solidAt = (px: number, py: number) => {
637	      const t0 = Math.floor(px / TILE), t1 = Math.floor(py / TILE);
638	      return st.inBounds(t0, t1) && st.isSolid(t0, t1);
639	    };
640	    let bounced = false;
641	    // X 轴：回退一步脱离实心 → 穿透轴是 X，反弹并退回
642	    if (!solidAt(this.x + this.w / 2 - this.vx, this.y + this.h / 2)) {
643	      this.x -= this.vx;
644	      this.vx *= -0.5;
645	      bounced = true;
646	    }
647	    if (!solidAt(this.x + this.w / 2, this.y + this.h / 2 - this.vy)) {
648	      this.y -= this.vy;
649	      this.vy = this.vy > 1 ? -this.vy * 0.5 : 0;

652	    return bounced;
653	  }

658	   *  随机、音量 ×0.5×style×0.05（style 6-10 → 0.15-0.25）、音高扰动 ±0.4
659	   *  由双素材近似）；≥5 消亡；全速翻面（与 lastVelocity 逐轴对比后取负）。 */
660	  private meowBounceOff(game: GameHooks): boolean {
661	    const st = game.world.store;
662	    const solidAt = (px: number, py: number) => {
663	      const t0 = Math.floor(px / TILE), t1 = Math.floor(py / TILE);
664	      return st.inBounds(t0, t1) && st.isSolid(t0, t1);
665	    };
666	    this.meowCount = Math.max(1, Math.min(5, this.meowCount + 1));   // Clamp(:16796)
667	    const style = 5 + this.meowCount;
668	    game.playSfxFiles([Math.random() < 0.5 ? 'Item_57' : 'Item_58'],
669	      0.5 * style * 0.05, this.cx, this.cy);
670	    if (this.meowCount >= 5) { this.killNow(game); return true; }
671	    if (!solidAt(this.x + this.w / 2 - this.vx, this.y + this.h / 2)) {
672	      this.x -= this.vx;
673	      this.vx = -this.vx;
674	    }
675	    if (!solidAt(this.x + this.w / 2, this.y + this.h / 2 - this.vy)) {
676	      this.y -= this.vy;
677	      this.vy = -this.vy;
678	    }
679	    return true;
680	  }
681	}

## 🤖 Assistant · 2026-08-13T08:50:21.459Z · glm-x-preview-260804

<details open>
<summary>💭 thinking</summary>

```
Key findings for Arrow.ts:
- `projSprite(projId)` at line 17-25: module-level `spriteCache` map. **CRITICAL POISONING BUG**: line 23 `spriteCache.set(projId, img);` — it stores the Image in the cache IMMEDIATELY (before load), and there's NO negative caching check — actually wait, the cache is set with the in-flight Image, not null. So subsequent calls return the same in-flight image object. That's actually OK for dedup, because the same Image object is returned and it'll complete later. But: **no negative cache** — if the image fails to load (404), the broken Image stays in the cache FOREVER, and `img.complete` will be true with naturalWidth === 0. Let me check: `if (img && img.complete && img.naturalWidth > 0)` at line 346 — so a failed image renders the fallback line forever. **Permanent failure caching = poisoning** per the iron rule. Also `img.src` has no error handler retry — but since the object is cached, it never retries. That's negative-caching-by-accident.

Wait, actually — more importantly: `projSprite` uses its OWN spriteCache, NOT `atlas.vimages`! So it doesn't share the atlas cache, doesn't get dedup with ensureVImage, and doesn't trigger onVImageLoaded. That's a **parallel cache** — a duplicate load path. Not "永不加载" though, since it does `new Image()` on first use. But the double-fetch: the same `Projectile_N.png` may be loaded twice (once into spriteCache, once into atlas.vimages if some other consumer calls ensureVImage with same path).

Let me note: line 16-25 projSprite: parallel module cache, first-call new Image triggers load, returns in-flight img. Fallback drawn while loading (line 348-356 short line). Self-healing: yes, next frame. Risk: 中 (parallel cache + permanent failure no-retry + no dedup with atlas).

Also line 39-58 `projFrameImg` — checks `img.complete && naturalWidth > 0` so safe.

Now let me look at Dart.ts more carefully — especially lines 176, 1016 which are still direct gets.

219	      // 尖刺球/巨石不随速度旋转,按位移滚转
220	      ctx.save();
221	      ctx.translate(this.x + this.w / 2, this.y + this.h / 2);
222	      if (st.spin) ctx.rotate(this.rot);
223	      else if (Math.abs(this.vx) + Math.abs(this.vy) > 0.01) ctx.rotate(Math.atan2(this.vy, this.vx) + (st.rotOff ?? 0));

225	      ctx.restore();
226	      return;
227	    }
228	    ctx.fillStyle = st.color;
229	    ctx.fillRect(this.x, this.y, this.w, this.h);
230	  }

153	    if (!perfect) {
154	      // :1347-1348 随机扰动：vy -= Next(10,31)*0.1 ∈ [-3.0,-1.0]；vx += Next(-20,21)*0.1 ∈ [-2.0,2.0]
155	      g.vy -= irand2(10, 31) * 0.1;
156	      g.vx += irand2(-20, 21) * 0.1;
157	    }
158	    g.rotation = 0;
159	    g.alpha = 0;
160	    // :1361-1374 粘滞与时档
161	    if (NON_STICKY_TYPES.has(type)) {
162	      g.sticky = false;
163	    } else if (type >= 375 && type <= 377) {
164	      g.sticky = false;
165	      g.alpha = 100;
166	    } else {
167	      g.sticky = true;
168	      g.timeLeft = GORE_TIME;
169	    }
170	    // :1391-1398 多帧族（Frame=SpriteFrame(1,rows)，行 0 起；1218 首帧随机行在 Update 内）
171	    if (FRAME_ROWS[type]) g.row = 0;
172	    // :1423-1425 SpecialAI==1（帆降）：初速整体覆写为 ((rand-0.5)*3, rand*2π)——
173	    // vy 兼作相位累加器，Gore_UpdateSail 据此走圆弧下落
174	    if (SAIL_TYPES.has(type)) {
175	      g.vx = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 3;
176	      g.vy = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
177	    }
178	    // :1442-1449（907-909 随机起始帧；本表未出现，保留语义）
179	    if (type >= 907 && type <= 909) g.row = irand(3);
180	    livePool.add(g);
181	    return g;
182	  }
183	
184	  /** 每固定 tick（Gore.Update，Gore.cs:313-1102） */
185	  fixedUpdate(dt: number, game: GameHooks): void {
186	    void dt;
187	    if (this.dead) return;
188	    const st = game.world.store;
189	    const t = this.goreId;
190	
191	    // sticky 入口：越界消亡 + 速度钳 32（:319-329 / DeactivateIfOutsideOfWorld :297-311）
192	    if (this.sticky) {
193	      const tx = Math.floor(this.x / 16), ty = Math.floor(this.y / 16);
194	      if (!st.inBounds(tx, ty)) { this.kill(); return; }
195	      const sp = Math.hypot(this.vx, this.vy);
196	      if (sp > 32) { this.vx *= 32 / sp; this.vy *= 32 / sp; }
197	    }
198	
199	    // SpecialAI 7（1218）：UpdateLightningBunnySparks :244-262（switch :331-345 早退）
200	    if (t === 1218) {
201	      if (this.frameCounter === 0) {
202	        this.frameCounter = 1;
203	        this.row = irand(3); // Frame(1,3) 内随机行
204	      }
205	      this.timeLeft -= vanishSpeed(t);
206	      if (this.timeLeft <= 0) { this.kill(); return; }
207	      this.alpha = Math.round(255 - (255 * Math.max(0, this.timeLeft)) / 15);
208	      this.x += this.vx;
209	      this.y += this.vy;
210	      if (this.alpha >= 255) this.kill();
211	      return;
212	    }
213	
214	    if (SAIL_TYPES.has(t)) { this.updateSail(game); return; }
215	
216	    // ---- 位移前物理（:352-769 链）----
217	    if (isDampRibbon(t)) {
218	      // :352-371 彩带阻尼
219	      this.vx *= 0.98;
220	      this.vy *= 0.98;
221	      if (this.vy < this.scale) this.vy += 0.05;
222	      if (this.vy > 0.1) this.rotation += this.vx > 0 ? 0.01 : -0.01;
223	      this.vy += 0.2;                 // 默认重力支（:760-764）
224	      this.rotation += this.vx * 0.05;
225	    } else if (SHRINK_TYPES.has(t)) {
226	      // :535-556 血块收缩（16/17 为 0.01 档——本表未出现，语义保留）
227	      this.vy *= 0.98;
228	      this.vx *= 0.98;
229	      this.scale -= (t === 16 || t === 17) ? 0.01 : 0.007;
230	      if (this.scale < 0.1) { this.scale = 0.1; this.alpha = 255; }
231	      this.resizeBox();
232	    } else {
233	      // 默认支（:760-764）：重力 0.2 + 随 vx 翻滚
234	      this.vy += 0.2;
235	      this.rotation += this.vx * 0.05;
236	    }
237	
238	    // ---- 碰撞/时档尾段（:929-1042）----
239	    if (this.sticky) {
240	      moveAndCollide(this, game.world, this.vx, this.vy);
241	      if (this.vy === 0) {
242	        // 落地摩擦（:1015-1029）：|vx|<0.01 归零
243	        this.vx *= 0.97;
244	        if (this.vx > -0.01 && this.vx < 0.01) this.vx = 0;
245	      }
246	      if (this.timeLeft > 0) this.timeLeft -= vanishSpeed(t);
247	      else this.alpha += vanishAlpha(t);
248	    } else {
249	      // :1041 非粘滞：两倍速渐隐
250	      this.alpha += 2 * vanishAlpha(t);
251	      this.x += this.vx;
252	      this.y += this.vy;
253	    }
254	
255	    if (this.alpha >= 255) this.kill();
256	  }
257	
258	  /** Gore_UpdateSail（Gore.cs:1231-1291）：相位下落；碰撞转落地滑移支 */
259	  private updateSail(game: GameHooks): void {
260	    const t = this.goreId;
261	    if (this.vy < 0) {
262	      // 落地滑移支（:1233-1261）：只推 velocity.X，不推进 position
263	      let moveX = this.vx;
264	      const size = Math.floor(Math.min(this.texW, this.texH) * 0.9 * this.scale);
265	      const probe = { x: this.x, y: this.y, w: size, h: size, vx: moveX, vy: 0.6, onGround: false, hitWall: false, hitHead: false };
266	      moveAndCollide(probe, game.world, moveX, 0.6);
267	      moveX = probe.vx * 0.97;
268	      if (moveX > -0.01 && moveX < 0.01) moveX = 0;
269	      if (this.timeLeft > 0) this.timeLeft -= vanishSpeed(t);
270	      else this.alpha += vanishAlpha(t);
271	      this.vx = moveX;
272	      if (this.alpha >= 255) this.kill();
273	      return;
274	    }
275	    // 下落支（:1263-1290）：vy 兼作相位累加器；UnitY.RotatedBy(vy) = (−sin, cos)，Y 取 |·|
276	    this.vy += Math.PI / 60;
277	    const dx = -Math.sin(this.vy) * 2 * 2;
278	    const dy = Math.abs(Math.cos(this.vy)) * 3 * 2;
279	    const size = Math.floor(Math.min(this.texW, this.texH) * 0.9 * this.scale);
280	    const body = { x: this.x, y: this.y, w: size, h: size, vx: dx, vy: dy, onGround: false, hitWall: false, hitHead: false };
281	    moveAndCollide(body, game.world, dx, dy);
282	    if (body.vx !== dx || body.vy !== dy) this.vy = -1; // 碰撞 → 落地支
283	    this.x = body.x;
284	    this.y = body.y;
285	    this.rotation = Math.atan2(body.vy, body.vx) + Math.PI;
286	    if (this.timeLeft > 0) this.timeLeft -= vanishSpeed(t);
287	    else this.alpha += vanishAlpha(t);
288	    if (this.alpha >= 255) this.kill();
289	  }
290	
291	  private kill(): void {
292	    if (this.dead) return;
293	    this.dead = true;
294	    livePool.delete(this);
295	  }
296	
297	  /** Renderer 统一绘制（Main.DrawGore Main.cs:21712-21742：中心锚 + scale + rotation） */
298	  draw(): void { /* 见 Renderer.drawGorePieces */ }
299	
300	  /** 多帧行数（1 = 整张贴图） */
301	  get frameRows(): number {
302	    return FRAME_ROWS[this.goreId] ?? 1;
303	  }
304	}
305	
306	/** 表项 → 具体 gore id（区间/候选均匀取值；不可求值跳过） */
307	function entryId(e: GoreSpawnEntry): number | null {
308	  if (typeof e.id === 'number') return e.id;
309	  if (e.idMin != null && e.idMax != null) return e.idMin + irand(e.idMax - e.idMin + 1);
310	  if (e.ids?.length) return e.ids[irand(e.ids.length)];
311	  return null;
312	}
313	
314	/** 表项 → 出生点（基准 = NPC 左上角；编译字段线性组合，未编译回退原点） */
315	function entryPos(e: GoreSpawnEntry, s: GoreNpcSource): { x: number; y: number } {
316	  return {
317	    x: s.x + (e.ox ?? 0) + (e.fx ?? 0) * s.w + (e.rx ? Math.random() * s.w : 0),
318	    y: s.y + (e.oy ?? 0) + (e.fy ?? 0) * s.h + (e.ry ? Math.random() * s.h : 0),
319	  };
320	}
321	
322	/** 表项 → 初始速度。语义 = NewGore 的 Velocity 实参整体替换（不含 NewGore 自带的
323	 *  随机扰动）：常量 > 均匀随机 > NPC 速度×k > 击退方向 > 继承 NPC 速度（缺省） */
324	function axisVel(v: number | undefined, k: number | undefined, m: number | undefined,
325	  a: number | undefined, hd: boolean, inherit: number, hitDir: number): number {
326	  if (v != null) return v;
327	  if (a != null) return uniform(m ?? 0, a);
328	  if (k != null) return k * inherit;
329	  if (hd) return hitDir;
330	  return inherit;
331	}
332	function entryVel(e: GoreSpawnEntry, s: GoreNpcSource): { vx: number; vy: number } {
333	  const hd = s.hitDir ?? 0;
334	  return {
335	    vx: axisVel(e.vx, e.kx, e.mx, e.ax, !!e.hd, s.vx, hd),
336	    vy: axisVel(e.vy, e.ky, e.my, e.ay, false, s.vy, hd),
337	  };
338	}
339	
340	/** 表项 ai 门（原版 `ai[N] == V`）：不满足跳过 */
341	function aiGateOk(e: GoreSpawnEntry, s: GoreNpcSource): boolean {
342	  if (!e.ai?.length) return true;
343	  return e.ai.every((g) => {
344	    const v = g.slot === 0 ? s.ai0 : g.slot === 1 ? s.ai1 : g.slot === 2 ? s.ai2 : s.ai3;
345	    return g.not ? v !== g.value : v === g.value;
346	  });
347	}
348	
349	/** 按表炸出某 NPC 的全部死亡 gore；add = 实体入桶回调。返回生成数 */
350	export function spawnDeathGore(s: GoreNpcSource, add: (p: GorePiece) => void): number {
351	  if (s.vanillaId == null) return 0;
352	  const list = deathGoreFor(s.vanillaId);
353	  let n = 0;
354	  for (const e of list) {
355	    if (!aiGateOk(e, s)) continue;
356	    const times = e.loop ?? 1;
357	    for (let k = 0; k < times; k++) {
358	      const type = entryId(e);
359	      if (type == null || type < 0) continue; // expr 留档条目（提取器不可静态求值）
360	      const pos = entryPos(e, s);
361	      const vel = entryVel(e, s);
362	      const scale = e.scale ?? (e.scaleExpr === 'scale' ? s.vanillaScale : 1);
363	      const g = GorePiece.newGore(type, pos.x, pos.y, vel.vx, vel.vy, scale, !!e.perfect);
364	      if (g) { add(g); n++; }
365	    }
366	  }
367	  return n;
368	}
369	
370	/** Enemy/Critter 死亡钩子：查表炸 gore（props 桶；表空 = 原版只出尘，返回 0）。
371	 *  hitDir = HitEffect 的 hitDirection（击退方向，`new Vector2(hitDirection, 0f)` 类表项用） */
372	export function spawnNpcDeathGore(npc: GoreNpcSource, game: GameHooks, hitDir?: number): number {
373	  const add = (game as unknown as {
374	    entities?: { add?: (p: unknown, bucket: string) => void };
375	  }).entities;
376	  if (!add?.add) return 0;
377	  const src: GoreNpcSource = hitDir === undefined ? npc : { ...npc, hitDir };
378	  return spawnDeathGore(src, (p) => add.add!(p, 'props'));
379	}
380

  draw(r: { canvas: HTMLCanvasElement }): void {
    const img = projSprite(251);
    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');
    if (!img || !img.complete || img.naturalWidth === 0 || !ctx) return;
    const a = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (255 - this.alpha) / 255));   // GetAlpha 亮度=255−alpha
    if (a <= 0.01) return;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.globalAlpha = a;
    // 绘制锚（Main.cs:29886 num143=18/num144=−10：贴图中心 = 盒中心 + (−10,−18)
    // ——段贴图骑在弹道上方而非盒心，2026-08-13 复查修正）
    ctx.translate(this.cx - 10, this.cy - 18);
    ctx.rotate(this.rot);
    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
    // scale 1.25（SetDefaults）——32×32 贴图 → 40×40
    ctx.drawImage(img, -20, -20, 40, 40);
    ctx.restore();
    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
  }
}

Good. The town NPC variant path:
- `n.townSheet` → `atlas.ensureVImage(sheetPath)` at Renderer.ts:4212. Good — lazy + self-healing.
- Fall back: `atlas.vnpc(n.vanillaId, n.frame)` at line 4222.

Now `vnpc` in SpriteAtlas.ts — line 220 `vimages.get(sheet)` for unregistered + new Image() manual load (bypasses ensureVImage → no onVImageLoaded, no dedup, no negative cache); line 234 `vimages.get(m.sheet)` for REGISTERED NPCs — pure direct get, NO load trigger.

Wait, let me re-read vnpc carefully:

```ts
vnpc(npcId: number, frameIdx: number): DrawRect | null {
    let m: VanillaNpcMeta | undefined = this.vanilla.npcs?.[String(npcId)];
    if (!m) {
      const key = String(npcId);
      m = this.lazyNpcMeta.get(key);
      if (!m) {
        const sheet = `vanilla/NPC_${npcId}.png`;
        const img = this.vimages.get(sheet);
        if (!img) {
          const im = new Image();
          im.onload = () => this.vimages.set(sheet, im);
          im.src = `sprites/${sheet}`;
          return null;
        }
        ...build meta...
      }
    }
    const img = this.vimages.get(m.sheet);
    if (!img) return null;
```

So for a registered NPC (in vanilla.npcs), it goes straight to line 234 `vimages.get(m.sheet)` — NO loading. If the sheet isn't preloaded → returns null forever → NPC invisible (falls to procedural fallback `enemySprites` in drawTownNPC line 4241). But `preloadMiscAndNpcs()` preloads all vanilla.npcs sheets. So registered ones are covered by preload. Registered = "纵向帧条 NPC 表（小动物等）" — preloaded at world load.

For unregistered: manual new Image() — triggers load, but:
1. **No dedup** — if vnpc called many times in one frame for same npcId before onload fires, multiple Image objects spawn. Actually no: after first call creates an Image and returns null, second call in same frame: `lazyNpcMeta` still empty, `vimages.get` still miss → creates ANOTHER Image. So N requests per frame until loaded. Minor inefficiency, browser dedups by URL in HTTP cache.
2. **No onVImageLoaded hook** — but NPC drawing is per-frame live, so no re-bake needed. Fine.
3. **No negative cache** — if the file 404s, it retries forever every frame! That's a **404 spin loop** for missing NPC sheets. Note the SpriteAtlas comment says NPC_Head 81-120 don't exist on disk — that's the NPC_Head family, not NPC_. But unregistered NPC ids where the file doesn't exist would spin.

Hmm wait — which NPC ids are unregistered? The comment at line 4107 says "589 在 vanilla-npcs.json 缺登记（json 缺口族，同 588/633/663）". So 588/633/663/589 are gaps. Do those files exist on disk? Let me check. Also which draw path consumes vnpc — drawEnemy presumably. Let me check the Enemy draw path and whether unregistered NPCs cause 404 loops.

Let me check public/sprites for NPC_588, NPC_633, etc.

1	// 原版 EmoteBubble（Terraria.GameContent.UI/EmoteBubble.cs）移植——NPC 头顶表情气泡。
2	// 素材 = Extra_48.png（272×1092，8 列 × 39 行，格 34×28：行0 列0/1 = 气泡框闭合/张开，
3	// 行 1+emote/4、列 emote*2%8+frame = 表情图标，每表情 2 帧动画、8 tick/帧）。
4	// 锚定（GetPosition Entity 分支）：位置 = 头顶 + (-dir*w*0.75, 2)，origin 底中，
5	// dir≠-1 时水平翻转（气泡尾巴指向 NPC）。
6	export interface EmoteBubbleInstance {
7	  npc: { cx: number; y: number; w: number; facing: number };
8	  emote: number;
9	  lifeTime: number;
10	  lifeStart: number;
11	  frame: number;
12	  frameCounter: number;
13	}
14	
15	const bubbles: EmoteBubbleInstance[] = [];
16	
17	/** 调试/探针：当前活跃气泡数 */
18	export function emoteCount(): number { return bubbles.length; }
19	
20	// 探针句柄：挂在 window 上保证与 Game/Renderer 同一模块实例（HMR ?t= 分叉时
21	// 动态 import 会拿到另一份模块实例，气泡数恒 0 的假象来源）
22	if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
23	  (window as unknown as { __swEmotes?: unknown }).__swEmotes = { spawn: spawnEmote, count: emoteCount, draw: drawEmotes };
24	}
25	
26	/** NewBubble(emoticon, anchor, time)：默认存活 180 tick */
27	export function spawnEmote(npc: EmoteBubbleInstance['npc'], emote: number, time = 180): void {
28	  if (bubbles.some((b) => b.npc === npc)) return; // 同一实体一次一个（原版 byID 近似）
29	  bubbles.push({ npc, emote, lifeTime: time, lifeStart: time, frame: 0, frameCounter: 0 });
30	}
31	
32	/** Update()（EmoteBubble.cs:177）：--life；每 8 tick 图标帧 0↔1 */
33	export function tickEmotes(): void {
34	  for (let i = bubbles.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
35	    const b = bubbles[i];
36	    if (--b.lifeTime <= 0) { bubbles.splice(i, 1); continue; }
37	    if (++b.frameCounter >= 8) {
38	      b.frameCounter = 0;
39	      if (++b.frame >= 2) b.frame = 0;
40	    }
41	  }
42	}
43	
44	/** 绘制（Draw :203-231 1:1）。img = Extra_48 表。
45	 *  ※在渲染器的世界变换内调用（ctx 已 scale(z)/translate(-cam)）——直接用世界坐标锚定 */
46	export function drawEmotes(ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, img: HTMLImageElement): void {
47	  if (!img || !img.complete || img.naturalWidth === 0) return;
48	  const CW = 34, CH = 28; // 272/8=34, 1092/39=28
49	  for (const b of bubbles) {
50	    const opening = b.lifeTime < 6 || b.lifeStart - b.lifeTime < 6; // flag：开合动画 → 行0 列0
51	    // 位置（GetPosition Entity 分支）：头顶 + (-dir*w*0.75, 2)px；origin = (格宽/2, 格高)
52	    const ax = b.npc.cx - b.npc.facing * b.npc.w * 0.75;
53	    const ay = b.npc.y + 2;
54	    const flip = b.npc.facing !== -1; // dir≠-1 → 水平翻转
55	    ctx.save();
56	    ctx.translate(ax, ay);
57	    if (flip) ctx.scale(-1, 1);
58	    // 气泡框：行 0，列 opening?0:1
59	    ctx.drawImage(img, (opening ? 0 : 1) * CW, 0, CW, CH, -CW / 2, -CH, CW, CH);
60	    if (!opening) {
61	      // 表情图标：列 = emote*2%8 + frame，行 = 1 + emote/4
62	      ctx.drawImage(img, (b.emote * 2 % 8 + b.frame) * CW, (1 + Math.floor(b.emote / 4)) * CH, CW, CH, -CW / 2, -CH, CW, CH);
63	    }
64	    ctx.restore();
65	  }
66	}
67

30	    opts?: { ai1?: number; ai2?: number; life?: number; noGravity?: boolean }) {
31	    super();
32	    this.x = x; this.y = y;
33	    this.vx = vx; this.vy = vy;
34	    this.damage = damage;
35	    this.knockback = knockback;
36	    this.projId = projId;
37	    if (opts) {
38	      if (opts.ai1 !== undefined) this.ai1 = opts.ai1;
39	      if (opts.ai2 !== undefined) this.ai2 = opts.ai2;
40	      if (opts.life !== undefined) this.lifeOverride = opts.life;
41	      if (opts.noGravity) this.noGrav = true;
42	    }
43	    const def = projectileData(projId);
44	    if (def) { this.w = def.width || 10; this.h = def.height || 10; }
45	    if (this.lifeOverride !== undefined) this.life = this.lifeOverride;
46	  }

49	
50	  draw(r: Renderer, _cam: Camera): void {
51	    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');
52	    if (!ctx) return;
53	    const img = projSprite(this.projId);
54	    ctx.save();
55	    ctx.translate(this.x + this.w / 2, this.y + this.h / 2);
56	    ctx.rotate(Math.atan2(this.vy, this.vx) + Math.PI / 2);
57	    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
58	    if (img && img.complete && img.naturalWidth > 0) {
59	      ctx.drawImage(img, -this.w / 2, -this.h / 2, this.w, this.h * (img.naturalHeight / img.naturalWidth));
60	    } else {
61	      ctx.fillStyle = '#D8C8A0';
62	      ctx.fillRect(-this.w / 2, -this.h / 2, this.w, this.h);
63	    }
64	    ctx.restore();
65	  }
66	
67	  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks): void {
68	    if (--this.life <= 0) { this.dead = true; return; }
69	    if (!this.noGrav) this.vy += GRAV;
70	    this.x += this.vx;
71	    this.y += this.vy;
72	    const tx = Math.floor((this.x + this.w / 2) / TILE);
73	    const ty = Math.floor((this.y + this.h / 2) / TILE);
74	    if (game.world.store.isSolid(tx, ty)) { this.dead = true; return; }
75	    for (const ent of game.enemies()) {
76	      const e = ent as unknown as { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; dead: boolean;
77	        hurt: (d: number, kx: number, ky: number, g: GameHooks, pen?: number, crit?: boolean, pierce?: number, fromPlayer?: boolean) => boolean; def?: { hitSound?: string[] } };
78	      if (e.dead || !projOverlap(this.x, this.y, this.w, this.h, e)) continue;
79	      if (e.hurt(this.damage, Math.sign(this.vx) * this.knockback, 0, game, 0, false, 0, false)) {
80	        playEnemyHitSound(game, e);
81	        game.addDamageNumber(this.x, this.y, this.damage, false, '#FFD060');
82	      }
83	      this.dead = true; // 穿透 1(城镇自卫弹均为 penetrate 1 或 -1 特化弹,近似单发)
84	      return;
85	    }
86	  }

5144	    const pulse = 0.22 + 0.14 * Math.sin(performance.now() / 180);
5145	    const tx0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor((cam.x - viewW / 2 / z) / ts) - 1);
5146	    const tx1 = Math.min(st.w - 1, Math.ceil((cam.x + viewW / 2 / z) / ts) + 1);
5147	    const ty0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor((cam.y - viewH / 2 / z) / ts) - 1);
5148	    const ty1 = Math.min(st.h - 1, Math.ceil((cam.y + viewH / 2 / z) / ts) + 1);
5149	    ctx.globalAlpha = pulse;
5150	    for (let y = ty0; y <= ty1; y++) {
5151	      for (let x = tx0; x <= tx1; x++) {
5152	        const i = st.idx(x, y);
5153	        if (!st.flags[i]) continue;
5154	        const t = st.type[i];
5155	        if (spel && Renderer.HIGHLIGHT_SETS.spelunker.has(t)) {
5156	          ctx.fillStyle = '#FFC419';
5157	          ctx.fillRect(x * ts + 2, y * ts + 2, ts - 4, ts - 4);
5158	        } else if (dang && Renderer.HIGHLIGHT_SETS.danger.has(t)) {
5159	          ctx.fillStyle = '#FF4040';
5160	          ctx.fillRect(x * ts + 2, y * ts + 2, ts - 4, ts - 4);
5161	        } else if (biomeS && Renderer.HIGHLIGHT_SETS.biome.has(t)) {
5162	          ctx.fillStyle = '#C060FF';
5163	          ctx.fillRect(x * ts + 2, y * ts + 2, ts - 4, ts - 4);
5164	        }
5165	      }
5166	    }
5167	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
5168	  }

```
Minimap skin findings:
- `minimapSkinAssets()` at Renderer.ts:5085-5098: per-skin lazy load. Each skin name cached in `minimapSkinTex` Map — one entry per skin, 4 images each. On skin switch: new entry created → 4 new Image() loaded. **No cleanup of the old skin's images** — all 9 skins × 4 = up to 36 images stay in memory once visited. Memory-only concern (each is small UI png). Not a correctness bug.
- `loadUiTex` line 5115-5119: `new Image()`, no error handling. If a skin's file is missing → broken Image cached forever in `minimapSkinTex` → **permanent negative cache, no retry**. Risk: 中低. And during pending: `tex.frame.complete` check at 5223 → frame not drawn (one-frame gap). Self-healing for the normal case.
- **Note**: this is a PARALLEL load path — bypasses `atlas.uiimages`/`ensureUiImage`. No dedup with vui's own cache. Risk: 低(结构性).

Now drawMapHeads — minimap NPC/Boss heads.

Fullscreen map:
- `MapBG{n}` via `atlas.vui()` — lazy (ensureUiImage), miss → dark fallback, self-healing. 低.
- `Map` (parchment) via `atlas.vui('Map')` — lazy, miss → not drawn (one frame). 低.
- `Extra_182` via `atlas.vui('Extra_182.png')` — lazy, miss → white diamond placeholder, self-healing. 低.

Hmm, one thing: `vui` uses `ensureUiImage` which has pending set but **no negative cache** (`_uiPending.delete(file)` on error, so a failed file will retry on next call — infinite retry every frame for a missing UI texture). Let me re-check:

```ts
private ensureUiImage(file: string): HTMLImageElement | null {
    const hit = this.uiimages.get(file);
    if (hit) return hit;
    if (this._uiPending.has(file)) return null;
    this._uiPending.add(file);
    const im = new Image();
    im.onload = () => { this.uiimages.set(file, im); this._uiPending.delete(file); };
    im.onerror = () => { this._uiPending.delete(file); };
    im.src = ...;
    return null;
}
```

Right — on error, the pending entry is deleted, so the NEXT call re-triggers a load → **infinite 404 retry loop** for any missing UI texture. That's the mirror-image of the "negative cache" in ensureVImage. Risk: 中 for vui if any consumed key doesn't exist on disk (MapBG indexing, Extra_182 key naming). Let me verify vui key naming: `vui('Extra_182.png')` — the uiFiles map keys. Let me check vanilla-ui.json key names to see if 'Extra_182.png' is a valid key, and whether `Map` is.

Let me check.

77	    num *= 0.25 + 1 * w.cloudAlpha;              // Rain.cs:28
78	    if (hooks.bossDanger) num = Math.max(1, num * 0.05);
79	    for (let i = 0; i < num; i++) {

83	      let x = camX - margin + Math.random() * (viewW + margin * 2);
84	      const y = camY - (20 + Math.random() * 80);
85	      x -= w.windSpeedCurrent * 15 * 40;          // 风偏移（Rain.cs:51）

183	  draw(r: Renderer, world: World) {
184	    const w = this.weather;
185	    const hooks = this.hooks;
186	    if (!w || !hooks || w.cloudAlpha <= 0) return;
187	    const ctx = r.ctx;
188	    const tex = getRainTex();
189	    const texOk = tex && tex.complete && tex.naturalWidth > 0;
190	    ctx.save();
191	    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
192	    for (const d of this.pool) {
193	      if (!d.active) continue;
194	      const tx = Math.floor((d.x + 4) / TILE);
195	      const ty = Math.floor((d.y + 4) / TILE);
196	      const [lr, lg, lb] = hooks.lightAt(tx, ty);
197	      ctx.save();
198	      ctx.translate(d.x + 1, d.y);
199	      ctx.rotate(d.rotation);
200	      if (texOk) {
201	        // 光照着色（原版 GetColor×0.85）：量化 8 级 × 3 型预烘焙乘色条（multiply+destination-in），
202	        // 每滴一次 drawImage——精确到 1/8 亮度量化，替代旧黑罩近似
203	        const lvl = Math.max(0, Math.min(7, Math.floor(((lr + lg + lb) / (3 * 255)) * 8)));
204	        const v = Math.round((lvl / 7) * 0.85 * 255);
205	        const tinted = tintedRainStrip(tex!, d.type, v);
206	        if (tinted) {
207	          ctx.drawImage(tinted, 0, 0, 2, 40, 0, -20 * d.scale, 2 * d.scale, 40 * d.scale);
208	        }
209	      }
210	      ctx.restore();
211	    }
212	    ctx.restore();
213	    void world;

218	const rainTintCache = new Map<string, HTMLCanvasElement>();
219	function tintedRainStrip(tex: HTMLImageElement, type: number, v: number): HTMLCanvasElement | null {
220	  const key = `${type}|${v}`;
221	  const hit = rainTintCache.get(key);
222	  if (hit) return hit;
223	  if (typeof document === 'undefined') return null;
224	  const c = document.createElement('canvas');
225	  c.width = 2; c.height = 40;
226	  const cx = c.getContext('2d')!;
227	  cx.drawImage(tex, type * 4, 0, 2, 40, 0, 0, 2, 40);
228	  cx.globalCompositeOperation = 'multiply';
229	  cx.fillStyle = `rgb(${v},${v},${v})`;
230	  cx.fillRect(0, 0, 2, 40);
231	  cx.globalCompositeOperation = 'destination-in';
232	  cx.drawImage(tex, type * 4, 0, 2, 40, 0, 0, 2, 40);
233	  rainTintCache.set(key, c);
234	  return c;
235	}

901	      let b = Math.round(255 * nightDim);
902	      // 天气联动：云染灰基准 = ProcessCloudAlpha（Main.cs:59154：max(cloudAlpha, 墓园强度×0.92)）×atmo
903	      const wr = this.weatherRef;
904	      if (wr) {
905	        const basis = Math.max(wr.cloudAlpha, (wr.graveyardVisual ?? 0) * 0.92);
906	        if (basis > 0) {
907	          const rainK = basis * 0.55;
908	          r = Math.round(r * (1 - rainK) + 158 * rainK);
909	          g = Math.round(g * (1 - rainK) + 162 * rainK);
910	          b = Math.round(b * (1 - rainK) + 168 * rainK);
911	        }
912	      }
913	      ctx.save();
914	      ctx.globalAlpha = c.alpha;
915	      ctx.translate(c.x + w / 2, c.y + h / 2);
916	      ctx.rotate(c.rot);
917	      if (c.flip) ctx.scale(-1, 1);
918	      // tint：先画原图再叠色保留形状（云端白色为主，直接 source-atop 着色近似原版 cloudColor）
919	      const tinted = this.cloudTint(tex, r, g, b);
920	      ctx.drawImage(tinted, -w / 2, -h / 2, w, h);
921	      ctx.restore();
922	    }
923	    ctx.restore();
924	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
925	  }

929	  private cloudTint(tex: HTMLImageElement, r: number, g: number, b: number): HTMLCanvasElement {
930	    const key = `${tex.src}|${r},${g},${b}`;
931	    const hit = this.cloudTintCache.get(key);
932	    if (hit) return hit;
933	    const c = document.createElement('canvas');
934	    c.width = tex.naturalWidth; c.height = tex.naturalHeight;
935	    const cx = c.getContext('2d')!;
936	    cx.imageSmoothingEnabled = true;
937	    cx.drawImage(tex, 0, 0);
938	    cx.globalCompositeOperation = 'multiply';
939	    cx.fillStyle = `rgb(${r},${g},${b})`;
940	    cx.fillRect(0, 0, c.width, c.height);
941	    cx.globalCompositeOperation = 'destination-in';
942	    cx.drawImage(tex, 0, 0);
943	    this.cloudTintCache.set(key, c);
944	    if (this.cloudTintCache.size > 64) {
945	      const first = this.cloudTintCache.keys().next().value;
946	      if (first !== undefined) this.cloudTintCache.delete(first);
947	    }
948	    return c;
949	  }

    if (this.fx.surfaceY <= 0) return;   // 未进世界（fx 未注入）
    this.ambViewW = viewW;
    this.ambViewH = viewH;
    const ticks = Math.max(1, Math.round(dtMs / (1000 / 60)));
    const wind = this.weatherRef ? this.weatherRef.windSpeedCurrent : this.wind;
    const moonlord = this.moonLordPresent();   // :1362-1369 月总天空冲突 → Ambience 禁出
    for (let f = 0; f < ticks; f++) {
      this.ambFrame++;
      if (this.ambNextAttempt > 0) {
        this.ambNextAttempt -= 1;                                   // :100-104
      } else if (!moonlord) {
        this.ambNextAttempt = 600 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 6600);  // :133 Next(600,7200)
        const a = this.amb;
        const fam = pickAmbientFamily({
          dayTime: a.dayTime, raining: a.raining, eclipse: a.eclipse, bloodMoon: a.bloodMoon,
          pumpkinMoon: a.moonEventKind === 2, snowMoon: a.moonEventKind === 1,
          zoneHallow: a.zoneHallow,
          zoneDesert: !!this.frameCtx?.scene?.zoneDesert,           // :77
          zoneBeach: !!this.frameCtx?.scene?.zoneBeach,             // :79
          zoneJungle: !!this.frameCtx?.scene?.zoneJungle,           // :84
          zoneCorrupt: !!this.frameCtx?.scene?.zoneCorrupt,         // :82
          zoneCrimson: !!this.frameCtx?.scene?.zoneCrimson,         // :83
          hardMode: this.hardMode(),                                // :61
          windyDay: this.windyDay(),                                // Main.cs:2976
          sunset: this.isSunset(),                                  // :39-46
          tooWindyForButterflies: Math.abs(this.weatherRef?.windSpeedTarget ?? 0) >= 0.4,  // NPC.cs:6801
          butterflyChanceLow: this.butterflyChanceLow(),            // :65
          playerAtSkyHeight: a.playerY <= this.fx.surfaceY + 1600,  // :190-193
          playerAtHellHeight: a.playerY >= (this.underworldLayerPx() - 100) * 16,  // :195-198
          moonLordActive: moonlord,                                 // :1362-1369
          roll5: Math.floor(Math.random() * 5),
          pick: Math.random(),
        });
        if (fam) this.spawnAmbientFamily(fam, camX, camY);
      }
      for (const e of this.ambEntities) {
        if (moonlord) this.ambientStartFadingOut(e, this.ambFrame); // :1383-1385 冲突即整体淡出
        this.stepAmbientEntity(e, this.ambFrame, wind);
        this.ambientEnvGate(e, this.ambFrame);
      }
      this.ambEntities = this.ambEntities.filter((e) => !e.dead);
    }
    if (!this.ambEntities.length) return;
    // atmo（Game.updateWeather 同公式，Main.cs:62622）——CommonDraw :76 color × atmo
    const atmo = Math.max(0, Math.min(1,
      (camY / 16 - (65 + 10 * (this.fx.worldW / 4200) ** 2)) / Math.max(1, this.fx.surfaceY / 16 / 5)));
    // GetColor 的 bgColor = Main.ColorOfTheSkies；以本帧天空底色近似（登记）
    const sky = this.lastSkyBottom;
    const sv = parseInt(sky.slice(1), 16);
    const sr = (sv >> 16) & 255, sg = (sv >> 8) & 255, sb = sv & 255;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
    for (const e of this.ambEntities) {
      const tex = this.ambTex(e.texKey);
      if (!tex || !tex.complete || tex.naturalWidth === 0) continue;
      // GetDrawPositionByDepth :85-88：(Position-相机中心)×(1/depth, 0.9/depth)+相机中心
      const wx = (e.x - camX) / e.depth + camX;
      const wy = (e.y - camY) * 0.9 / e.depth + camY;
      const sx = wx - camX + viewW / 2;
      const sy = wy - camY + viewH / 2;
      if (sx < -400 || sx > viewW + 400 || sy < -400 || sy > viewH + 400) continue;
      const fw = tex.naturalWidth, fh = tex.naturalHeight / e.rows;
      const scale = (3 + e.depthScaleAdj) / e.depth;   // CommonDraw :79 + 各族 Draw depthScale 偏移
      // GetColor（各族覆盖）：
      //  常规 = Lerp(bg, 白, BrightnessLerper)（:165-168）；
      //  gastropod = Lerp(bg, (102,0,63), 0.75)（:658 + Colors.cs:39）；
      //  hellbats = Lerp(白, 灰, Depth/15)（:871，无 atmo 乘）
      //  群系背景层 alpha 乘子（Vulture/Seagulls/Bats/EOC 族 ×bgAlphaFrontLayer）无对应态，按 1 近似（登记）
      let cr: number, cg: number, cb: number;
      if (e.kind === 'gastropod') {
        cr = sr + (102 - sr) * 0.75; cg = sg * 0.25; cb = sb + (63 - sb) * 0.75;
      } else if (e.grayDepthFade) {
        const k = Math.min(1, e.depth / 15);
        cr = 255 + (128 - 255) * k; cg = cr; cb = cr;
      } else {
        const b = e.brightness;
        cr = sr + (255 - sr) * b; cg = sg + (255 - sg) * b; cb = sb + (255 - sb) * b;
      }
      const alpha = Math.min(1, e.opacity * (e.grayDepthFade ? 1 : atmo));
      if (alpha <= 0.01) continue;
      ctx.save();
      ctx.globalAlpha = alpha;
      ctx.translate(sx, sy);
      ctx.rotate(e.rotation);
      if (e.dir < 0) ctx.scale(-1, 1);   // SpriteEffects.FlipHorizontally
      ctx.drawImage(tex, 0, e.frame * fh, fw, fh, -fw * scale / 2, -fh * scale / 2, fw * scale, fh * scale);
      ctx.restore();
    }
    ctx.restore();
    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
  }

212	  private loadBg(ids: number[]): Promise<void> {

214	      if (this.imgs.has(n)) return resolve();
215	      const im = new Image();
216	      im.onload = () => {
217	        this.imgs.set(n, im);
218	        // decode():背景图 2048px 级,首帧 draw 才解码会卡一拍——预载阶段完成解码
219	        im.decode().then(() => resolve(), () => resolve());
220	      };
221	      im.onerror = () => { this.imgs.set(n, im); resolve(); };
222	      im.src = `sprites/vanilla/Background_${n}.png`;
223	    }))).then(() => undefined);
224	  }

Sfx synth switch covers: hit, chop, hurt, killed, pkilled, zombie, roar, pickup, dig, tink, place, drink, splash, summon, whipCrack, explosion, coin, door_open, door_close, shatter, drown, mirror, conch.

The explosion case was explicitly fixed ("曾无此分支"). So the remaining ones have the same first-play-silent pattern. Especially: **thunder** (weather conditional — first thunder of a rainstorm is silent), **bowShoot/throw/gunShot** (very frequent, but only first play), **beeSummon** (boss summon — one-shot, so if the first-ever play is silent it's a missed cue; but subsequent summons work since buffer cached).

And the ensureBuffer pending semantics: pending set prevents concurrent fetch, but note `pending.delete` happens in `.finally` — correct. And failed is permanent negative cache — so a transient network failure = permanently silent for that sound (until reload). That's a semantic choice.

One more important thing: `ensureBuffer` calls `this.ensure(true)` — creating the AudioContext on first miss. In some browsers, creating an AudioContext before a user gesture leaves it suspended → decodeAudioData may still work. Minor.

  applyPowder(projId: number, x: number, y: number, w: number, h: number): void {

      const y0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(y / TILE) - 1);

  }

  playSfx(name: string, vol?: number, x?: number, y?: number) {

  startTrackedLoop(name: string, vol: number, x: number, y: number) {

  }

  playSfxFiles(files: string[], vol?: number, x?: number, y?: number) {
    if (!this.sfx.playFiles(files, vol ?? 1, x, y)) this.sfx.play('hit', vol ?? 1, x, y);
  }

  /** 按原版文件名直播（无合成兜底——音色精确的场景如蜂后吼/仪式圈）：
   *  缓冲未就位时触发后台懒加载，本播静默（下次命中真声） */
  playSfxFile(file: string, vol?: number, x?: number, y?: number) {
    this.sfx.playWavFile(file, vol ?? 1, x, y);
  }

  /** 武器使用音（2026-08-13 特效音效审计）：Item.cs SetDefaults 显式 UseSound
   *  优先（vanilla-itemusesound.json 582 件全量提取，含 Item_2 镰刀/Item_8 恶魔镰
   *  Item_12 激光枪/Item_43 魔镜反弹/Item_92 水矢 等），无显式回落各路径原通道音 */
  playUseSound(vid: number, fallback: string, vol?: number): void {
    const wav = itemUseSoundWav(vid);
    if (wav) this.playSfxFile(wav, vol);
    else this.sfx.play(fallback as 'throw', vol);
  }

  /** 矿车碾过压板轨道（Minecart.HitTrackSwitch → Wiring.HitSwitch，Minecart.cs:1287-1294）：
   *  路由到 Wiring.hitSwitch——压板/轨道族直接 TripWire（Wiring.cs:167-171） */
  hitTrackSwitch(i: number, j: number): void {
    this.wiring?.hitSwitch(i, j);
  }

That's the explosion-sfx lesson partially addressed: playSfxFile callers are silently skipped on first play.
